- Where did you find this wallet?
- I found it in the garbage at the corner of that street.
- What did you do with that garbage to see the wallet, by the way?
- I removed it.
- Are you working as a waste collector? Because to me,you don’t look like that. Come here and work as a security guard for me. A few days ago, I had my pocket picked. Maybe, they took all the money and threw the walled into the garbage. Fortunately, you found it. Thank you very much. Please come here tomorrow to get the job if you want.
- Thank you so much. You are very kind, but I already have a good job.
- You mean, as a waste collector?
- No sir, I teach at the Polytechnic University, but in my free time, I often pick up garbage and trashes to keep our street clean so that foreign visitors won’t have a bad impression of our country.
- eh?
- I have to go now.
A girl came and said:
- Could you please give me your phone number? I would love to get to know you
He gave her his phone number and left.
The girl said to her father:
- Daddy, could please tell your private detectives to find out information about this man within a week for me.
The father asked:
- What are you planning to do?
- I would like to know clearly about this person before making a decision.
- What decision?
- You always urge me to get married, but I haven’t found a right person yet. Oh, it seems I found one already.


18. In the rain
Two people sat under a porch in the street to avoid the heavy rain.
The girl said:
- Since I joined your Trash-lovers Association to pick up trash on the street, I’ve suddenly understood many things. Now I can enthusiastically do many things that I was afraid of before. I thought that I was a wealthy lady, so I didn’t have to get my hands dirty. But when I do those things, I actually have a lot of fun.
The man smiled:
- I returned the wallet to your father and didn’t expect to meet and have an excellent member of the Trash-Lovers Association.
- That day you made me curious about a good-looking and high educated guy but is passionate about… trash. I fell in love with that man… From now on, I will be more active in the Association to understand you more and more.
- Joining Hoi Yeu Rac is against the daily routine of people: we won’t easily throw away unusedstuff but must try to find a way to recycle,reuse it effectively. It’s not an easy thing.
- I know it will be tough but it is all worth it to me. Nowadays, it is very hard to find a man like you that actually places the national honor above everything. I love, respect and admire you for that. So, from now on, I will spend some time every day to pick upgarbage with you for a Garbage-free life. I will love Garbage because I love ...
The girl hesitated to continue, asthe rain was falling slightlyout there.
The man kindly said:
- I have a big dream and ambition! It is to clean up and remove all the trashes in the world, and enlighten everyone about Trash. Only when we share the same goal and dream, we can really understand each other. Anyway, It’s late now. Let me take you home. The street starts to flood.
- Don’t call a taxi. It is not that far from here. Shall we walk home in the rain?.
He nodded and smiled at her. 
- Look, garbage is blocking the drains,the water cannot escape that worsen the flood.
The girl seemed thoughtful, and so did the man. They realized there are many things that they have to do.


19. Sins and blessings with Trash
In a rainbow cloud, a little angel knelt asking his Master:
- Dear Master, I saw that Mr. Thong in that house was living a good and moral life. Hedid a lot of good deeds and never did anything wrong, but why couldn’t hego to heaven after death?
The Master replied:
- Blessings and sins are not easy to understand. In fact, trash prevented him from going to heaven. Since he was rich, he easily threwmany things away, including the good stuff. He created so much trash that it became his barrier to heaven. Thus, he was born into a wealthy family but must do something related to Waste Management to pay his debt to the environment.
The little angel thanked his Master and asked:
- Dear Master, so why a guy named Mau who committed a crime of robbery andmurder wasn’t condemned to hell after death for punishment?
- That guy was sentenced to 20 years in prison, and when he was relieved, he didn’t know what to do for a living, so he chose … Trash. He took trash then carefully sort it manually. Every week, he sold cleaned plastic bags, fermented fertilizer, scrap paper/metal, etc. He treated most of the trash from a dump with his hands.Thoughhe already earned a living from that work, it also accidentally created good karma for him. So when he died, King Yama praised his actions, forgive his mistakes, and let him be reborn as a nice person.
The little angel thanked his Master and said
-After listening to you, I also like to come to the human world to do Trash business.
The Master burst out laughing:
- Silly you!
Then they flew off.

20. Plastic garbage
Two policemen stopped their car, looked at the address, and asked the security guard:
- Hello, is this the home of Mr. Tuan, the plastic producer?
- Yes sir. May I ask what’s the matter? My boss is honest and has never done anything illegal. He only produces cheap plastic bags for everyone and pays full tax.
A policeman said:
- We know that. We are not here to arrest him or anything. We just have an important thing to tell him.
The security guard let the two policemen see Mr. Tuan.
A policeman said:
- Please calm down and listen to our announcement. This morning, your daughter and her classmates went to the river to play. She accidentally fell into the river. The rescue team urgently sought and found her trapped under a garbage dump drifting across the river. However, the garbage pile was huge and tangled, that why they could not find and rescue her in time. It was too late when she was rescued. We are terribly sorry. Please accept our deepest sympathy.

21. Too late
The eldest brother slammed his hands on the table and angrily said:
- Why didn’t you arrive in time to pay respect and say goodbye to our late mother? She got buried already. It takes you only 3 hours to come here by boat.
The younger brother said:
- Dear brother, hearing our mother died, I hurriedly loaded my boat to come here. But on the way, my boat’s propeller got stuck in the plastic garbage that made the engine died, and the boat drifted freely. I dived into the water to get rid of thestuck garbage. And when I finished, the boat already reached the estuary. Halfway when I rushed back, my boat ran out of gasoline. So, I had to dock my boat and ran to find a gas station. And when I already bought and brought back the gas, my boat was stolen. It took a few days for the police to find and get my boat back. – He was bursting into tears.
The eldest brother then looked at his sister:
- How about you?! Why are you late? Does your car have a propeller that got stuck in the garbage?
The sister whimpered:
- Brother!It was not my case.My stupid driver wound down the window and threw away a plastic bag on the road. The police chased my car and discovered that he had afake driving license. So I ran into trouble afterward, and it took me a few days to solve a lot of legal problems. It turned out that the driver was a successful garbage collector. However he found that being a driver would be better, so he changed his career with a fake driving license. Luckily, he didn’t hit anyone. – She was sobbing
The eldest brother looked over the youngest brother:
- And you? How could garbage stop your plane? Why are you late?
The youngest brother sorrowfully said:
- Dear brother, when the plane flew a third of the way, then suddenly there was an awful smell, and the passengers aggressively reacted to it. The flight attendants tried to find the source of the smell and found that it was from the bag of an absent-minded old man. He mistakenly took a plastic bag of garbage instead of his clothes. Unhappily, there was a dead mouse inside. The captain had to fly back to land to resolve the incident. It took me 6 hours to get back to the plane, so I’m late for our mother’s funeral…
The oldest brother plopped down and said:
- It was my fault that I didn’t clean up the house carefully enough. There were still some trashes. Our mother slipped on a banana peel, failed, got brain injury then faint and death. It was all my fault!!

22. Because of Trash
The landlord asked Phong:
- Phong, why do you look so pale and sick?
- Because of the trash. I live 5 km away from the landfill and still get poisoned with the polluted water and air. That's why I always get sick.
The landlord then asked Ly:
- Ly, how about you? Why do you look so healthy?
- Thanks to trash. There was an old landfill which heavily polluted the soil and water.The city was struggling to deal with it. So I was hired to treat that million of tons of trash at a reasonable price. I had it sampled and researched for a year. After found out the appropriate process to treat those trashes,I dug it up and bring it to the factory next door to process it very effectively. Moreover, I also found that many complex components in the buried trash are extremely valuable. I have made a lot of money from both cleaning the trash and selling tons of recycled material. My lifehas changed! I have only processed nearly haft of the landfill, but I am rolling in money. I come here to see you guys because I miss you. If any of you needs a job, I can help.
Many friends admired him very much. The landlord then asked Lan:
- Lan, you look so beautiful! How about you? How have you been?
- I also live by doing trash business like Ly, but to a lesser degree. I don’t do business in this world anymore but in the clouds! (They all laughed) So I work on the network. I have a service that says "If you have something unwanted, call us." So people all over the country call me. My people come to buy it at a price close to zero. I fix and decorate it then divided those into two parts. One is to donate to poor people in remote areas and needy students. The second part is for people who want second-hand items for its lower prices. For over ten years, I have done business and do a lot of charity with the unwanted things. Who knows that trash could be a tool to make money, right?! So, I’m not very rich but have enough money to burn Ly up. (Everyone laughed) 
The group cheered admiringly. The landlord then asked Minh:
- Oh Minh, why do you have a big bruise on your face?
- Because I was volunteering as a vigilante.
- To catch robbers?
- Oh man, with this skinny body? No. Whenever I see a garbage bag in front of a house waiting for garbage collectors, I will talk to the owner and persuade him/her not to do so because it makes the street look ugly. I convinced them to only put the trash out when the collector arrived or put it in a nice trash can. Some listen to me, and some don’t. Yesterday, I talked to the man who was arguing with his wife. He didn’t dare to argue with her, so he vented his anger on my handsome face.


23. Son-in-law-to-be contest.

Many young men were waiting to be called in for the interview to become the son-in-law of a very rich businessman of the city. They all looked all shy and nervous. The door opened, a disappointed man came out and left right away without saying goodbye to others. One more opponent got weeded out. In turn, each person was called then went out quickly, failed. It was so hard.
This future father-in-law was the boss of a big business and had only one daughter, no mistresses and illegitimate children. He was undoubtedly a rare and precious kind in this world. Anyone who became his son-in-law would together with his daughter receive all the inheritance. More than 200 applications were applied to be his son-in-law.
The daughter was ridiculous, too. She didn’t want to choose her husband but let her father do it for her. With the personality like that, it was not easy to be her husband. But who cared about romantic love, a good fortune was way more than enough.
So, all focused were on the father but not the daughter.
A young man standing at the gate begged a candidate who had just failed:
- Please tell me the interview questions.
- He asked me: “What will you treat things you don’t want to use anymore?” I quickly responded: “I throw them away” And he told me that many people had the same answer as me. He also said that I would be invited to the next interview if his wife gave birth to another daughter, but everyone knows his wife was 55 and so hard to get pregnant at this age. Just a polite way of saying you fail. So I said goodbye to him and left.
That young man asked another man.
- What were his questions for you?
- “When can you actually stop using something?” I said: “When it's broken.” Then he said, “thank you and see you next life.”
That young man asked another man.
- I was asked: “What is the most recent item that you do not use anymore?” I was shocked. After a few seconds, I answered it was my motorcycle. He said the camera told him that I had just gone to the toilet, i.e., my urine. How can we answer such a question?
A week later, no one has been selected because the questions were so random and tricky. Nobody could google or do any research to prepare for an answer.
The daughter smiled:
- With your such questions, I think I will be single forever, dad.
The father said:
- I don’t want to do it the hard way, but through their way of treating trash, I can assess their loyalty. It seemed that no one love and care enough for trash. They don’t know you, and it appeared that they don’t care about you, but only me, although they will marry you. You brought them water and fruits, but no one realized you. What a pity.
The girl smiled:
- What kind of answer that you have been looking for?
- I would love this kind of answer. “Sir, for me, nothing should be thrown away. Everything has a way to be used or reused. As long as we have the love for everything, including things like trash, then there will be nothing to be thrown away.”
The girl burst out laughing:
- So you should see the members of Trash-Lovers Association, and I’m sure you will find your son-in-law.

24. Super Evil
On the top of Mount Hoa Son, the Devil opened the Evil contest to find one succeeding to his throne, so that he can retire. However,his throne couldn't be vacant because as long as there are evil people, there must be the evilest as the top manager.
After seven days passed, the jury took four evilest people to the Devil. The king gazes his evil eyes at the four and said:
- Of four of you, who is the evilest?
One came out and said:
- Your Majesty, I am. I destroyed all my opponents and took the throne. As a king, I killed most of the people in my country. If the neighboring country didn't attack to liberate my country in time, there would be no people in my country.
The Devil nodded:
- You seem eviler than me.
The second one came out and said:
- Your Majesty, I am eviler than that guy. He is cruel, and everyone knows that, but none knows my cruelty. I always prove myself a gentleman who protects people everywhere, but in fact, I instigate all countries to fight with each other, so that I can sell weapons to them. Many many people died but they still fell grateful to me.
The Devil nodded:
- I cannot be compared with you.
The third one came out and said:
- Your Majesty, those two guys cannot be compared with me. I produce cheap products with poison infused in the chips installed inside. When the world gets poisoned, they will no longer be able to have babies. I don’t kill anyone, but in the end, this world is only occupied by my people.
The Devil trembled with fear saying:
- Happily, you haven’t yet set foot on my palace, or I cannot stay here now.
The fourth one came out and said:
- Your Majesty, how long can those guys survive to rule this realm of Evil? I have provided the world with cheap plastic material since a longtime ago, and now plastic waste floats everywhere in rivers and the sea. When it decomposed into the water and soil, the plastic is absorbed into the flesh and blood of every species on Earth. You, sir, also use a lot of plastic things, and they are in your blood now. No one sees the immediate danger of plastic, so they keep using it, making the world extinct gradually. Your body is now filled with plastic, so your gesture and voice start to get plasticized.

The Devil angrily shouted:
- You are the super evil.

25. Lessons learned from Trash
The final kick knocked Alan up and unconscious. He was carried to the emergency room. After a week, he was discharged. Texting or calling or leaving a message on Facebook couldn’t help him contact his girlfriend. Everyone gave him a forced smile when seeing him. The knockout has closed the door to his success in his career and life. Being so hopeless and disappointed, Alan left the city and decided to live the rest of his humble life in the suburban area. He thought his life would be like trash from now on.
One time, when wandering on the steep slope, he met an old man pulling a heavy cart up. Alan helped him to push up his cart. However, the horrible smell started to bother him
At the top of the slope, Alan asked:
- Uncle, what’s in your cart that smells so bad?
The old man replied:
- I take trash home from the landfill.
Alan said:
- Oh, what for?
- Many precious things are in the trash but nobody knows. I don’t have enough energy to do many things out there, but I take trash, sort it, and treat it accordingly. Some for compost and some for scrap or recyclable materials. Through that, I can make enough to afford my living. You got to know that in this life, nothing is worth for you to throw away. Everything has its value as long as you have the heart to see that.
Alan kept watching the old man went away.
The next month, Alan opened the martial art and morality classes for children in the neighborhood. The parents could pay;however, they want or even none for their children to attend. The people in the area that have kids going to Alan’s class were very pleased because their children became much more well-behaved and healthier. Alan felt happier than ever. It is true that nothing in life should be thrown away. As long as one has a good heart, everything is valuable and precious. 

26. Extreme caution
Alan sat with the old man looking at the river. The old man said:
- Scavenging in this garbage dump is very risky, so we must be very vigilant. We don’t know what we will have. It can be an expensive watch, a gold chain, a wallet with only papers inside, a decomposing fetus, or an arm. Everything! From precious stuff filthy wasteof human. From this pile of unwanted things, we try to find recyclable things, that is, we contribute to reducing the amount of waste in life. There should be no free garbage dumps in a progressive country, but perhaps this rural countryside is not as civilized yet. You told me that you used to be a boxer and the kick of the opponent forced you to retire from the ring. I think that you got knocked out because you weren’t cautious enough. When competing, we must predict and be vigilant about all the moves and blows of the opponent. If you miss one, you will be screwed.
Alan looked excitedly at the old man.
- Oh, you know martial arts too?
The old man pulled Alan up and asked him to react that competition. Alan recounted the details of each blow of the opponent. It’s true that the opponent had an unpredictable kick.
The old man said:
Here’s a deal! Every day, you can come here, and I will attack you in many ways. You will learn to be vigilant and aware all the time. Whenever I cannot beat or attack you anymore, and you never get surprised by my blows, you have mastered this skill. 
Alan came to practice with the old man every afternoon. Unbelievably, the old man was an expert. He had many strange and flexible moves and blows that Alan couldn’t predict. In the first month, Alan always got hit since he couldn’t speculate about the old man’s moves and blows. In the second month, Alan was hit less because of his vigilance.
By the fifth month, Alan couldn’t be hit anymore because he had always been alert to the old man’s every move and blow.
Alan knelt and told the old man:
- I don’t want to compete with anyone anymore, but Master, please let me be back into the ring once more to see and test how far I have learned. 
The old man paused a little bit and then nodded. 
At the ring, cheers resounded, lights shone all over the ring, and the competition was announced through thespeakers.
Alan touched the opponent’s hand to greet him according to the rules. The referee signaled to start the game.
His practice indeed rewarded. Alan was able to correctly predict of all the opponent’s blows, so he could avoid all of them and saved his energy. At the final round, Alan dodged the opponent’s attack and came closer to hit his chin and knocked him up. Since Alan did not hit the opponent very hard, he was not being knocked out or seriously hurt, but hesurrendered because of exhaustion. He had no energy left to fight.
After winning the fight, Alan returned to the countryside and continued his work of teaching martial arts and morality to all the kids in the village. He also called for cooperation in managing waste and recycling. All garbage trucks from everywhere were allowed to dump at one place in the village, and all the people here would process those and made money from it.

27. Election
In a podium in front of thousands of people, a candidate started her speech:
- Hello everyone!If you vote for me in the next term, I promise to contribute to building a healthy society without low-income families, unemployed people. I promise to make sure that nice people willnever be disappointed, children that can always go to school, peasants never losing their crops, the state budget never being lost, delegates never break their promises, love never dies, a true and delicious Pho (Vietnamese Beef Noodle Soup) never stop being delicious, our soldiers will never lose in a battle, fake news never spread in social networks, contaminated food will never be sold, absent-minded people never get lost, drunk neighbors will never scream at night, government agencies will no more treat people badly. Generally, our society will be much better and less chaotic!
After a long and “promising” speech, the candidate stopped to wipe his sweats.
Meanwhile, a voter raised his hand to give his ideas:
-With our due respect Ma’am, I think that you failed to mention many much more serious problems that we are facing in our community. Trash and garbage are still scattered everywhere on the street. Noise pollution from many shops and vendors with annoyinglyloudspeakers are still not banned. We still fail to ban and eliminate the wasteful use of plastic bag. We still fail to teach our students in school about environmental issues, about keeping our city clean, about trash management and recycling. We still fail to keep our street and city clean! Imagine, in this modern and civilized time, we still let our city and countries look nasty because of littering and trashes. Whose fault is that? Is it because those politicians can never keep their promises? So, if you want us to vote for you, make sure you hear us clearly and keep your promises. We want a nice and clean city. We want schools to have the program to teach children the responsibility to keep our city and environment clean. We want that there will be more activities to raise awareness of environmental issues in the communities and to inspire people to keep the streets clean. Those are our wishes! If you’re not determined to do these things, we guarantee that you cannot go far.  
The candidate eloquently replied:
- If you entrust me with these tasks, I won't let you down. Please vote for me and you will see everything will change day by day.




28. Intense hatred
Mary cut the seagull’s belly then pulled out all of the plastic jars, plastic bags, and plastic clips. Tom said:
- Look! There is also a fish bone, that is, this bird had eaten a fish dying of plastic things and later died before of those plastics too.
Mary sadly said:
- The life of this world is dying out of plastic waste, but unfortunately, no one is determined to do anything to stop it.
Tom said:
- Plastic waste brings death to the world very slowly. That’s why no one is afraid of yet, and when people are, it’s too late.
Mary said:
- Very true! First, it is because of the cheap production of plastic stuff. Since then, sellers always pack and deliver their products with plastic bags. Plastic waste, then, flooding everywhere. And since it is so cheap, nobody cares to recycle it or use it responsible enough. Furthermore, leaders do not have enough knowledge and skills to deal with this plastic waste efficiently. Thus, at the end of the day, all the plastics are pushed down to the lowest place, which is the ocean. Ocean is the source of life. Destroying the ocean will bring the disaster to the lives of many species on this planet. The consequences are unpredictable. What should we do now, Tom?
Tom answered:
- To make such change, one must have enough knowledge and power. Right now, very few people will listen to us. But when we have power, our orders must be executed. But in this time of democracy, no one has an absolute power to do things that they want. Law and orders are controlled by different forces. So it’s not easy to fight against plastic waste.
Mary said:
- Well! We don’t completely oppose plastic waste, but we oppose the massive production of plastic stuff. We oppose the refusal to reuse plastic bags, the wasteful way to use plastic. We oppose when unuseable plastic items don’t get recycled. We are against dropping plastic waste into the rivers and the sea. We are against people’s negligence and ignorance about this serious issue.
Tom laughed and said:
- Then you oppose almost all the world, Mary! To do what you just said, you must become the Master of the World.
Mary said:
- I am not qualified for such a position and don’t like it anyway. But to the day I die, I will work with world leaders, inspire and convince them to be more aware on this issue and fight those issues for me. I will also educate, inspire, and convince the young generation, who will be soon in charge of this world, to fight against plastic pollution.
Tom asked:
- How?
Mary answered:
- I cannot do it alone, so I will invite many people to participate in education and raising awareness about plastic waste and other environmental issues. Eventually, all the leaders must take action, and they will use their power to make some change!
Tom said:
- It might take a long time, sweetheart.
Mary said:
- I know. But I have pledged to dedicate my life to work and pursue this. If you cannot support my dream,you can find happiness with another girl. No hard feeling!
Tom smiled:
- Don’t be silly! I will do it with you because I also wish to do something useful for society. Personal happiness is short. When you live for everyone, our joy will last much longer.
Mary looked at Jon and smiled…
- Are you sure?

29. Revenge
They searched for a while and then gave up. They talked to each other:
- Obviously, he ran here but why couldn’t we find him?! What if our boss asks about him. That little boy is so stubborn, and his eyes are like fire. If he’s alive, he’ll find us to revenge.
The driver of a garbage truck pressed the horn to ask for the way. They dodged but did not forget to look back and gave the truck driver the evil look. That was the story of fifteen years ago. Now, Bao, also with his gun, went into an abandoned warehouse followed by his fellow officers in their uniforms.
Gunshots, screams, footsteps could be heard everywhere. Bao said through a small walkie-talkie attached to the collar:
- Brothers, let handle these subordinates, and I will chase after their boss. I counted the gunshots and knew for sure that he has used all of the bullets. Now he's running away only with a knife.
- Yes, sir.
Bao ran around to catch the boss because he knew his way. Bao jumped over the low wall and kicked the head of the boss. The boss rolled up several times then stood up with a shiny knife.
Bao put the gun in its holster then gently unbuckled the gun belt, took off the armor, and put them in a corner. He stepped toward the boss, stood in fighting stance and without any weapon.
The boss swung his knife as fast as lighting to attack Bao. Bao calmly and quickly avoided his attack.
When the boss was about to give Bao an extremely dangerous jab in his left chest, Bao dodged into the other side and use his right shoulder to push the knife out of the boss’s hand while kicking him on his belly. The boss was hurt by Bao’s move. He laid there, bleeding and immobile on the concrete floor. He still held the knife but was unable to move because of all the pain.
Bao stood up near him, look at him in the eyes and said:
- Fifteen years ago, you killed my whole family. I jumped into the garbage truck and hid in the garbage. You felt disgusted so you gave up searching for me, so I survived. I became an orphan and a scavenger like other poor children to make a living. However, I never stopped wanting to revenge. I lived frugally and changed my name to go to school. I had to sneak to visit my parents’ and siblings’ graves because I was afraid that you were waiting to catch me. When I saved enough money, I applied and was admitted to the police program that specialized in Criminal Investigation. After the graduation, I spent a lot of time searching for you. The good thing is that all gangs related to one another, so I soon found you. I filed for the case. I am the leader, so I have many rights to take action. The hard part is that you bought off some police guys, which makes it harder to catch you. It took me a long time to fool the guy who provided you with the information and lead my team directly to attack you and your gang today. I'd love to let the law executes you, but your lawyer will have enough words to help lessen your sentence. So I will do it myself.
The boss suddenly stood up and ran towards Bao. As expected, Bao dodged to catch his hand and then turned the knife towards his chest and stapped him in the heart. 
Bao came to pick up his armor and gun. The teammates rushed to him. He quietly came to his car in tears.
Bao put flowers on graves and cried.




30. Faithfulness
The commander pointed at the screen with a stick and said:
- We’re done with this case. The killer was arrested, the evidence was sufficient, and the file has been transferred to the prosecutor. The Minister proposed to reward us. Today we summarize this case to get experience. So here is the case…
Duong started to recognize the strange attitude of his girlfriend named Tuyet, so he secretly watched her. As Duong guessed, Tuyet fell in love with another man named Hung. Hung was richer and better looking than Duong. The pain and hatred were consuming all of Duong’s mind. That night, Duong told Tuyet that he had earned a large sum of money and wanted to give it to her. Tuyet believed so, and they had a date at a hotel. Inside a hotel, Duong blamed Tuyet for her unfaithfulness. Tuyet accused Duong of his irresponsibility and uselessness. Duong got angry and strangled Tuyet until she was motionless on the floor. He was panicked and tried to cure Tuyet, but he had never learned about resuscitation, hence failed to save Tuyet.
Duong felt regretful, so he was laying down and hugging Tuyet’s body all night.
The next morning, Duong went to buy some plastic bags and a sharp knife. He cut Tuyet into many pieces in the toilet then put them in small bags. Late at night, he took those bags out and threw them to the garbage dump 10km away.
Duong returned the room to clean the traces carefully, checked out and then left. Duong also unplugged the sim and battery out of Tuyet’s cell phone.
Tuyet’s family reported her disappearance to the police. During the investigation process, Duong tried to act like he was in a great pain and actively involved in the search. The exciting thing is that he had a really good acting skill, even better than Tony Leung, so the investigation team left him out of suspicion. He answered questions about where and when very reasonably. But, our team secretly followed him for a week and found that he went everywhere to ask about the case.
When we came to a dead end, a scavenger discovered a few human fingers whose nails got painted. We scavenged through the garbage and found more plastic bags with decomposed body’s parts inside. We hid this news and asked the people around to keep it quiet.
The DNA test showed that it was Tuyet. We took the garbage dump as a center to started to investigate all the motels around that area to look for traces. We came to each, and the owners of those motels said that they didn’t see anything unusual for the last couple weeks. However,our police dogs discovered the smell of Tuyet’s blood in a room. We asked to review the security cameras to see who was the renter half a month ago. A man like Duong came and left at night on two consecutive days.
After knowing this, we came back and did everything we could to make Duong confess.
After the director of the police team discussed the case, the room discussed excitedly for the rest of the meeting. The director drank some tea and said something that seemed irrelevant. "Even this and many other cases were solved, we see that unfaithfulness in a relationship has caused many severe crimes."
A female police asked:
- Boss, please help us define faithfulness.
The director twiddled his glasses and said:
- Faithfulness is when you have an opportunity to be in love with another person with who has some certain advantages, but you still determine to be with your current partner forever.
The whole room applauded for the boss's profound philosophy. Another staff said:
- Please let me say in reverse order, boss. Faithfulness is when you determine to only keep a relationship that you currently have even when you have an opportunity to have a better one.
The commander said:
- I like how you said it! By the way, poor the garbage dump. There is a rumor that people heard some girl was crying there everynight. That’s why nobody wanted to stay there after sunset anymore. 

31. Garbage crisis

The streets of Barcelona resounded with screams and shouts. People protested because the trash was filling up the city and the government was very slow in taking the trash out. The foul smell filled the air. Garbage trucks’ drivers also protested and struck. While more and more garbage were accumulated and not taken out, goods and food were still brought to the city regularly. The residents dumped their trashes every day, but no one took the garbage out of the town.
At first, people were just annoyed with garbage, next offended, and then they felt sick and stressed, which made all the work stagnant. The life of people here was like to reach the end of the world.
The union that handled waste management business was one that ordered a strike. City authorities urgently negotiated with them. It appeared that they didn’t reach an agreement. The union asked the mayor to resign, and they would promote the guy named Alfonso to take his place.
It turned out that trash could actually interfere in politics.
Some people rent the truck to have the garbage dump closed to their house taken away. However, some gangsters tried to stop them and beat the hell out of those drivers. It turned out that the many gang groups also involved in waste managing. 
While the city went crazy with garbage, there was an article that was quicky spread on social network. Here is its content:
- We create Trash! We turn goods and food and practically everything into trash at the speed of a thousand gazelles.
- We are irresponsible to the Earth. We turn the precious material of the Earth into disgusting waste. In other words, we turn the Earth into something disgusting and filthy.
- The crisis of the waste management is the warning of Nature for all of us. Nature wants us to change our lifestyle and our attitude toward garbage. Perhaps, we should consider garbage as our good friends,not our enemies.
- Protesting does not solve any problems! So instead of forcing the government to process our garbage, we should do it by ourselves. We are completely able to do so!
- We must live the lifestyle that creates very little trash or not at all trash. With the trash that already there around us, we must sort, clean, and dry it, then find a recycling center to turn it into the nearly original materials that we got from Earth.
- Everyone must involve in waste management to save themselves rather than force the government to do for them.
When that article reached everyone, and people read, though, and started to discuss it.
One morning, some people took their spades to the garbage. They tried to sort and dry it. Other people started to do the same as well. The city turned into a waste management ground where every resident was a sanitation worker.
After sort and dry trash, people used their cars to carry cleaned and treated garbage out of the city and donated it to factories which needed raw materials. For garbage that didn't get recycled yet, they were put in an empty area and wait for the government to help to process those.
The gang couldn’t do anything to stop it because all the people in the city participated in such activities. Political pressure also naturally disappeared.
After a few days, people wrote this sentence in front of their houses: Barcelona will no longer create garbage.

32.Female Terrorist
Hearing the alarm went off, Phil watched the screen and found a shadow climbing the fence to access the factory. The second screen showed that person successfully unlocked the door of the office. Phil said to Cam:
- Call the police for the support, and I myself go to arrest this person. Why does this person look like a girl with something on the back?
Phil took a special stick out of the security control room. He spoke over the walkie-talkie:
- Cam! Please see on the screen and tell me the location of the intruder so that I can catch him.
- Yes, sir.
Phil came from behind the intruder and seized his collar. The girl screamed because of being startled and she immediately bent to escape and elbowed Phil’s belly making him fall back. The girl quickly took off her backpack and threw it through the door of the one room and ran away.
Phil tried to run after her and kicked her back. She fell on the corridor. Phil rushed to lock her hands. The police car just arrived with the siren wailing.
In the interrogation room, the investigator asked:
- Why do you try to bomb the factory gathering plastic waste from the sea?
The girl shyly said:
- Because of my boyfriend...
- Did he make you do this?
- Oh no, just because he swore that…
The investigator looked at the camera at the corner of the room, knowing their colleagues were watching them in the next room.
- Repeat it. What did your boyfriend swear?
- Once we stood by the sea, he took an oath that he would love me forever unless all mountains collapse, all rivers are dried up, and the sea no longer has plastic garbage. Now people build factories to clean the sea by taking all the plastic garbage from it. I fear that when the sea is clean, my boyfriend won’t love me anymore, so I planned to bomb the factory…

33. Naive
- Daddy, where this candy will go after being eaten?
- Oh, it will be dissolved to feed the body with nutrients.
- What does it feed the body for, daddy?
- So that we will continue to live, sweetie.
- We will continue to live for what?
- So that we can go to school, work, and play.
- We go to school, work, and play for what, daddy?
- To go to school like you so that when you grow up, you can go to work like me and go to the market like mommy.
- When I grow up, I will go to work like you for what, daddy?
- To serve the society, country, and community.
- To serve the society for what, daddy?
- So that everyone can go to school, work, and play like us.
- Oh, I understand. To live is to go to school, work, and to play. What about this candy’s packaging? Where will it go?
- To the trash can, sweetie.
- Then where will it come, daddy?
- Garbage trucks will pick it up and dump it in a garbage dump.
- The garbage dump will be as high as a mountain because of the accumulated garbage?
- People will burn some and incinerate some so that it won’t be too high or stinky.
- What if someone doesn’t put the packaging in a trash can? Where will it go?
- Then it will go to sewers, rivers, then the sea.
- Does anybody pick up garbage on the ocean to keep it clean?
- No one has done it, or very few.
- If there is too much garbage in the ocean, can fish still swim? Is there anything wrong happen to them, daddy?
Mommy interrupted:
- Then you will receive lashes. Why do you keep asking your dad all the silly questions? Stop it!
- So if the fish cannot swim, I will receive lashes, won’t I, mommy?
- Of course, keep on asking, you will be spanked!!
- What’s wrong with me if I am spanked? When I grow up, can I go to the sea and pick up all the trashes there, mommy?
- No, you must become a doctor.
- No!!  I don’t want to. I can’t stand giving shots to babies. I will pick up garbage in the sea for fish to swim.
Turning to daddy, the little boy said:
- Where should I put the garbage after picking it up from the sea?
- Give it to mommy, and she will sell it, and we have money to buy fish to eat.
- Can we do it, daddy? If so, why do people let garbage drift on the sea?
- Because people haven’t been good at recycling plastic garbage. At the time when you grow up, people will be better at it, and plastic waste will be valuable assets. Those who have much plastic waste will be rich.
- So those who collect plastic garbage in the sea will have huge wealth, is it true, daddy?
- That's right. At that time, people will scramble to pick up plastic garbage.
- So, we should rush to collect it right now, right daddy?
- Mommy has not allowed us yet
- So Mom is the boss here, right dad?

34. Finding A Wife
Lan hugged and kissed his parents on his their cheeks then hugged his brothers, sisters and said goodbye to them. Afterward, he drove his motorcycle away. The father shouted to him:
- I don’t care if your future wife is beautiful or rich, I just hope that she has a good heart! 
Everyone in his family is married, and only Lan is so picky that he is still single. His family always urge him to get married and have children.
Lan is nearly 40 years old and has a successful career. He has a company that produces super-clean fertilizer for organic farming. His products have great values and competitive quality, not only within Vietnam but also worldwide. However, he is still single. Though many girls want to be his girlfriend, he has refused all.
Lan wanted to take a few months off to have a roadtrip across the country with his motorcycle. That’s all he wants to do. However, he also does not want to make his parents worried. Plus, he is extremely respectful to his parents. Thus, he made up a reason for this random roadtrip by saying that:
- Because you want me to get married, so I go to seek a wife. I already assigned my job to my assistant and I will supervise them from far.
His parents were pleased to hear it and asked him to bring his girlfriend back.
Lan happily went sightseeing everywhere that he only heard of. However, he didn’t forget his actual duty: Finding a wife.
His father asked him over the phone:
- Have you found her?
- Not yet, dad. I went to some places, but the streets were still dirty with a lot of garbage.People live there seem like they do not care to keep their communities clean. So, I bet all the girls that live there are the same kind and they are not my type. 
- Don’t be too harsh, my son! 
Lan continued his journey.
His father phoned him:
- What’s up, my son? Any updates yet?
- Good news dad!  I just found a very clean town. I think people that live here are actually good and civilized. I will stay here for a while to find out more about the town and the people and hope to find a good daughter with a good family!
He stopped by a tea house to have some tea and got to know more about this town!
He was told that Mr. Hai’s family is a role model of this town. He convinced the villagers to keep the road clean and not to throw the garbage out to the road. If there were a trash out on the road, he would find out who had dropped it and make them pick it up. Ever since, everyone here tried to keep the village clean and neat. He has a grand daughter who just graduated from a university. She is very nice and pretty. Many families here want her to date their sons, but she refused. She is very picky. She wants her husband to be a responsible, educated, and moral man. She also wants him to have a super clean toilet in his house, a nice garden, and his neighborhood with no trash.
His father called him:
- Any updates yet?
- I’ve found one, but it will take some time to get to know her and win her heart. In the meantime, you must convince and encourage everyone in our neighborhood to keep our town clean.
- Ok! I will pick up litter in our area every day. It will be too late if we mobilize everyone.
- Tell everyone in our family to do that with you if they want me to have a wife, dad.

35. The Sherlock Homes Burglar

The burglar sneaked into the garden then used a master key to unlock the front door and stepped inside. He went into the bedroom and took all the garbage and put them in a bag. After that, he went into the kitchen and livingroom and also took all the garbage from there. In each bag, he labeled the room in which he took the trash from.When he was in the backyard, suddenly the home owner arrived at the gate. He had to lie down behind a bench for half an hour. When everything was okay, he climbed up the wall to escape and came to another place.
After arriving, he dropped all the garbage in front of the girl.
He pointed out each item with a stick and start to analyze the… trash:
- From this, we can see that he cares deeply about his appearance because there are a lot of empty bottles of lotion. He’s also very wasteful because those bottles are still more than half. However, he can be very kind and generous to poor people, as we can see there are a lot of lottery tickets that he bought from them that were thrown away right after being bought. He doesn't have time to cook and often eats ready-made food that was delivered to his house. He probably also has an anger issue, because of these broken bowls. He prefers to write than to type because of these two pens out of ink, but I don’t know who he wrote to.
“There are also two kinds of flowers here, probably the bought those flowers for 2 different women,” the thief hesitated.
The girl sobbed.
- Please stop!! How can I love such a person? He looks good but… It’s so true that by looking at ones’ trash, we can tell exactly who they are. I should break up with him though I haven’t known him for long.
The thief surprised. He paused a little bit to think. He felt some what guilty and responsible for the break-up. He then reluctantly tried to defend that guy:
You know we should not be so quick to judge… This sunscreen bottle shows that he quite cares about the environmental issue. The heat of the sun and their ultraviolet rays are very harmful and can even cause skin cancer. He is considerate and he will probably take good care of his family later.
And these half-empty containers maybe were expired. But if it is his wasteful nature, then only you can change him.
He probably does not know how to cook but likes to eat at home. You two can happily cook together.
When I came to his house yesterday, I forgot that I also saw a cat. Maybe it is the cat that broke these bowls.
I am a man but still like those who love flowers, and I think they are quite romantic. How can you break up with such a person?
Her tears hadn’t dried up yet but she started to smile.
- You are so right! He is such an amazing person. I never pick a wrong person!
It’s true that women fall in love with their ears. 


36. Blind
A group of medical inspector came to give checkups to a village to assess their health. The result indicates that the health of the people are quite good, perhaps because the environment there is not much polluted. But the strange thing is that most people here have eye diseases and some are blind without clear explanations. The examiners report to the Health Department to ask for experts to come to find the reason.
Many groups of experts came but still couldn’t find the cause. They tested the water, soil, food, gene, clothes, tears samples, etc. but didn’t see anything unusual.
The eye doctor Ton That Theu felt discouraged. He wandered by himself at the end of the village. He saw an old monk with his walking stick picking up trash on the road in front of the Buddhist temple. The doctor gently greeted the monk then passed by. The monk called him:
- Doctor, what’s troubling you?
The doctor surprisedly turned back and asked:
- How do you know I’m a doctor?
- By your glasses, clothes, pen, and face. You look like a doctor! 
- With those such things? How great! Dear monk, we came here to study the eye disease here, but we haven’t found the cause yet. I’m really discouraged.
- The monk said:
- Because you always look for external reasons but not the internal ones.
The doctor surprised.
- Internal reasons?
- There are two types of blindness or blurred vision; it is either pathological or psychological types. You are looking for the causes of pathological blindness but not psychological ones.
- Could you please be more specific?
- For example, what have you seen since you came here?
Dr. Ton That Theu said:
- Dear monk, trees, and houses as usual.
- See. You are also psychologically blind because you didn’t see the garbage all over the village. People here don’t know that garbage damages the landscape. They’re used to seeing trash everywhere and don’t feel annoyed anymore. That is psychological blindness. Though their eyes open, they cannot see anything. Apart from the garbage, they also don’t see the poor people around them to help. That psychological blindness is the bad deed that leads to the result of physical blindness which cause cannot be found through scientific examination. So instead of testing samples, let educate people! Help them to see the trash so they can pick up the trash; help them to see many poor people around them so that they can offer help. When they overcome the psychological blindness; when they are able to see and care more for the communities, the eye disease in this town will go away.
Dr. Ton That Theu gazed at the monk in amazement.

37. The Notebook Of The Mafia Boss. 
He was vomiting blood. His blood splashed all over the bed and almost filled up a basin. Everyone panicked for awhile but then gave up when they saw him taking his last breath. He was gone. They burst into tears.
His body was bathed, put on makeup, dressed in beautiful clothes, then put in the open coffin. The servants hurriedly prepared for the funeral.
In the particular room, the oldest son stonily said:
- Dad had a notebook recording all the codes, accounts, passwords, and the lists. Those are all the top secret information; that is why he never entered them into a computer. As a rule, he had to hand it over to me before he died. I had something to deal with and didn’t return in time to see him. When I arrived, he already vomited blood and then died. The notebook hasn’t been given to me yet, and I do not know where he put it. So you all have to find the notebook and don’t let anybody know. In 3 days if you cannot have it, I will execute one by one. Dismiss.
Everybody anxiously found the notebook all over the house. The oldest son and servants took care of the funeral.
The youngest daughter stood silently thinking. Then she went into his father’s room to observe. The room was clean wholly and quickly by servants.
She asked a servant:
- Who was cleaning his room?
- Four people in total.
- Call all of them.
The servants described their work in details. Then she asked them to go to the garbage can.
But sanitation workers had taken it already.
She called three assassins to follow her to the garbage factory. She asked the people there where the last garbage truck go. It went to the garbage in the suburbs. She rushed there. The system was operating. It sorted various types of metal waste and then transfer to the incinerator to run the generator. She asked where the garbage had just been dropped, and the staff pointed to the pile as big as a mountain. She asked them to stop all their operations. The factory manager went out and shouted at her. The assassin grabbed him by his collar and slapped him, and he passed out immediately on the floor. All the staff was frightened and rushed to stop everything as she ordered. Other garbage trucks had to dump the garbage at somewhere else.
She asked people to shine floodlights at the pile of garbage as big as a mountain. Then she, herself, raked, picked, and looked carefully at every piece of the trash. The three killers guarded her at a distance.
Nobody expected that a beautiful and petite girl like her also has a great health. She kept on scavenging until the next morning but didn’t seem tired at all. Of course, the three trained assassins didn’t seem tired, either.
Finally, she found a bloody pile of garbage that had a notebook covered in blood. In turned out that her father held this small notebook in his hand, but no one noticed. When he vomited blood, his notebook fell into the basin. After his death, the servants cleaned and poured the basin into the garbage can with other garbage, and the garbage truck dumped it here. Her guess was right, and she found the notebook there. She quietly opened the notebook and read then secretly used the tiny camera on her ring to capture each page.
She carefully wrapped up the notebook and then called the three killers to leave, but didn’t forget to throw a significant amount of money to the factory staff.
As soon as the car arrived in the courtyard, the mansion was surrounded by three helicopters and police cars.
Five years later she was released from prison. Nothing left. The notebook and her ring were also confiscated when she was arrested. Her siblings were still in jail.
She came to the garbage factory to look for a job. The factory workers didn’t know anything about the arrest five years before because it was kept quiet from the media. They happily gave her a job because they still remembered the girl that can scavenge through trash like her before.
She worked hard and had many initiatives that help the factory gain more profit. She said:
- My scavenging on that night enlightened me about trash. There are many precious materials in it. Trash is a significant material and material is the resource. If we sort trash carefully, we can exploit more useful material from it. The old way to treat trash was very wasteful.
A few years later, she became the factory’s director. She expanded many activities and grew rich from her careful treatment of trash.





38. The Snakehead Fish
Little Bin cried out:
- Daddy! Look! There are many baby snakehead fishes following their mommy in this pond!
Khai came shortly.
- How strange! Usually, when a mommy snakehead takes her babies to seek food, they must move continuously, why this family keeps hanging around here?
Khan used a fishing net to see if there was anything unusual down there. It turned out that the big mommy fish was trapped in a plastic bag and couldn’t escape it. That was why all the baby fishes kept swimming in one place. 
Khai laughed happily.
- This fish is perfect for the Vietnamese Sour Soup!
Little Bin grabbed the plastic bag and set the fish free. He cried:
- If you cook the mommy fish, nobody will take care of all the baby fishes!
Khai angrily said:
- What are you talking about? It’s for our soup.
Little Bin cried:
- Without the mother, the babies will be eaten by other large fish. They will be safer with their mother.
Khai spoke softly:
-Don’t be so silly, sweetie! 
If someone catches you and mommy for their soup, how can I survive? (He sobbed)
Khan laughed then took his son and the fishing net home.
Little Bin said:
- I watched TV and saw a bird gets stuck in a plastic bag, a turtle in a plastic mesh, and today a snakefish in a plastic bag. Plastic bags are very harmful to animals. How could we ban and stop all the use of plastic bags, daddy?
Khai smiled wryly.
Little Bin said:
- Every time mommy returns from the market, she brings many plastic bags. I don’t know where those bags throw away, but the sea is now full of plastic bags. Don’t go to the sea, dad, or you will be stuck and die in a plastic bag.
Khai smiled wryly.
Little Bin then said:
- When I grow up and finish school, will you buy me a boat? I will go to the sea to pick up plastic bags to save fish and turtles.
Khai smiled wryly.
Little Bin said:
- Or you and I should become those killers like the ones in Korean movies, and we will kill plastic bag makers, and that’s it.
Khai couldn’t bear any longer, he said:
- Stop sounding like an old fart, want to be in prison, boy? 

39. Driving
Mr. Phuoc Nguyen put the letter on the table then remained silent. His wife read the letter quietly.
"Dear Nguyen Van Phuoc, Principal of the Super-Fast Driving School.
First of all, I would like to introduce myself. I am Tran Van Toi. I have a vendor that sells iced tea by the road that your student drivers pass by every day.
I have endured them for so long, waiting so long, before writing to you. And as soon as this letter arrived at your house, its content has also been posted on Facebook by Trash-lovers Association.
Dear Sir, 
I think when you choose students, you must choose the ones who are already somewhat educated. After you get their driving tuition, you must teach them driving theory, traffic law, driving ethics, and then let them practice driving a car.
I think when you choose students, you must choose the ones who are already somewhat educated. After you get their driving tuition, you must teach them driving theory, traffic law, driving ethics, and then let them practice driving a car.
Honestly, I don’t know what the heck you have taught your students. (I am utterly sorry for the use of blunt language here) Every day your students drove by my place, they always threw out plastic bottles and bags through their car windows and even some still with ice cubes inside. They do it every single day. 
My vendor is on the route that many foreign visitors often pass by. Frankly speaking, seeing your driving practitioners throwing plastic bags, bottles out on the road, I am so humiliated, I just want to cover and hide my face. That is indeed an embarrassment to our country.
So I must have someone look after my vendor while I go to pick up plastic all of the trashes littering by your students. But selling iced tea doesn’t earn much. I cannot even afford a quality meal to have enough energy to go after your students to clean up their mess. Thus, I only picked up garbage on a short distance and must come back to my iced tea stall so my helper could leave. I am in great pain when thinking about plastic bags and bottles scattered on other roads.
When my girlfriend broke up with me because she found out that I was only a man who sells iced tea for a living but not the director of a big company, I was sad for only three months. And now, five years have passed, the pain caused by your students’ garbage has never stopped.
I ever thought of stopping my ice-tea-business so that I wouldn’t have to witness your students throwing plastic garbage out on the road anymore. But who would take my place of selling super fresh and clean and organic iced tea in this busy city?
My ice is made out of water that meets national standards. The tea is from organic gardens and free from any chemicals or fertilizers. The water is filtered by the most modern filtration system. My iced tea is very safe and clean. If possible, I invite you to come to my vendor to have a cup of my special tea. 
I opened my heart to you through this heartfelt letter, as sincere as one could be. I hope you have a way to improve this situation because if not, I think that drivers coming from your driving school ​​will be littering all the streets of our beloved homeland.
Please send my regards to your family.”
After reading the letter, the wife said:
- This letter is tactful, isn’t it? What are you worried about?
Mr. Phuoc Nguyen said:
- I think that it would be a waste if we let one who has great writing skill and a witty sense of humor like this guy to sell iced tea. We should ask him to work at our school. If he’s not married, we could introduce our daughters to him

40. Super Liar
Four guys were having a drink. They didn’t know what to do for fun in the meantime, so they held a Super Liar contest. The winner wouldn’t have to pay for his beer for tonight at all. 
The first person told his story:
- Last week I was promised that if I could ask a hundred people to protest, I would receive a Mercedes as a reward. I paid each person 300,000 VND and thought I would earn a good deal of money. But unexpectedly, after the protest, the promise wasn't fulfilled so I ended up losing more than 30 million VND that I had taken from my wife. I was a liar, but the one who promised me was even a bigger one. I do not dare to go home because I know my wife will be asking me about a new Mercedes and her 30 million VND. (He burst into tears)
Three friends nodded sympathetically.
The second person told his story:
- Your failure is also mine, because if you were successful, then I would become the President. I was, oh damn, promised that if the protest were successful, people would come to take me to the presidential palace with a limousine. And now I’m here with you guys, and my several billion VND was transferred to other organization.
- Is it so? How sad I feel.
- I bet it's true one hundred percent. The super liar is the guy who took all my money but doesn’t let me become the President.
The other three men nodded sympathetically.
The third person told his story:
- I feel sorry for you two guys. You were tricked, while I benefitted from it. After the protest, the streets were full of trash. I felt miserable looking at it, so I decided to pick it up. Guess what I picked up? A bundle of lottery tickets from a protester! I won the lottery with all these tickets. I will take the prize tomorrow morning and will share with you to help you feel better.
- Thanks for being kind to us, but what is a lie here?
- It is that I can hide this story from my wife. You know the husband must give all his money to the wife, but my wife hasn’t known anything about these lottery tickets so far. Ha! Ha!
The three friend congratulated him!
Finally, it was the fourth person’s turn. Suddenly his phone rang. He continuously said “yes, yes” happily on the phone. Finishing, he turned to tell his friends.
- I have to go home now. My wife just told me that when she scavenged through a trash can, she found the lottery ticket of Vietlott that wins the first prize of 95 billion VND.
He then ran away.
Three people looked at each other awkwardly:
- Did he tell the truth or a lie?
A server came to give them the check, and they suddenly realized that guy was the super liar because he ended up tricking his friends to pay for his beer.

41. The Bloody Battle

A disciple ran in and said:
- Master, there is a very strange man with his garbage cart arrived at our martial art school and wants to challenge you. All other students have tried to stop him, but they were all knocked down.
The old master frowned and went out with his sword.
The scene was chaotic. Broken tables and chairs were everywhere, and all of his students cried in pain. The master calmly asked:
- Who are you and why did you do it? We don’t have any feud. Why you were so cruel to my students?. Actually, what do you want?
A strange man, in ragged clothes and massive beard with a bamboo stick, grumpily said:
- Stop talking. Just fight with me. If I lose, I will go away and never come back, but if you lose, you must hand over this school of martial arts to me.
The old master hissed:
- You have gone too far. Watch this!
The master drew his sword, bent his body, and flew to the weirdo with three consecutive moves. The stranger swung his bamboo stick to attack back as quickly as lightning. His bamboo stick received the force from him, so it was as hard as steel and couldn’t be cut by a sword.
The two old men vigorously to fight one another. All the people at the school gazed at them with astonishment.
Bam!!! 
The two men hit each other and bounced back, vomiting blood. They tried to stand up with their sword and stick. It seemed like they couldn’t continue their fight.
A moment later, the master asked:
- Who are you?
The weirdo said:
- It seems like no one could end this battle. Cause I won’t leave, and you won’t hand over this school to me. Thirty years ago, you and I once had a competition on top of Mount Thien Nhai to decide who would marry sister Hong and succeed our Master in his school of Martial Art.
The master exclaimed:
- So, you must be brother Thai. Please forgive me for not recognizing you. You have changed so much.
A strange man continued:
- Lac!! You lured me to the edge of the mountain and pushed me down with your internal energy. You thought that I was dead, so you went home and married Hong and took over the master's school. But you should have known that like in all kung fu movies, no one dies from falling into an abyss. However, I wasn’t saved by humans, but I was saved garbage. It turned out that at the bottom of the abyss was the landfill of four villages nearby. Falling in a bed of trash, I passed out for a feel days but not dead. I woke up and felt grateful to the garbage, so I decided to stay there. I used plastic garbage to build a small hut and use the decomposed waste to grow vegetables. Rice seeds in the garbage also grew into rice trees.
I lived in that garbage abyss for 30 years while practicing Yin-Yang qigong of Phat Quang Quyen and waited for the right time to revenge. When I felt I was strong enough, I flew to the ground to live with people. I make a living by collecting garbage. I love garbage so that I set up a waste management company and became wealthy, but I still get dressed poorly as a way to remind me about the feud with you that I have to revenge. 
Today I come here to take what should have belonged to me! My love - Hong and this martial art school. Unexpectedly, you have diligently practiced martial arts and progressed very fast. So I guess, it is not as easy as I thought. 
I will come back at another time.
Suddenly…
- Thai !! It’s me!
There was a beautiful old lady ran from inside to the strange man and held him tight. They were both sobbing. All of the students in the school were very surprised.
The old lady said in tears:
- I have waited for you for so long.  I’ve never stopped loving you. Lac and I didn’t marry. When you disappeared, I ran away and tried everything I could to find you. Even Lac always wanted to marry me, but I refused to be his wife. I let my sister, Nguyet, marry him instead, so that I can stay single and wait for you…. 
42. Less waste
It was the Less Waste Generation contest, and the winner would receive the brand new fancy motorcycle from the Director of the Trash-lovers Association. She bought it not so long ago, but she decided to give it to someone who could give a brilliant idea to minimize waste. 
Years after years, no one had won this contest, so the director continued to use that motorcycle until it became old. She then announced that who won the third prize would have that old bike. That time, none of the members won the award, either.
How strange! There were so many young trash-lovers who easily had unique ideas, but why they didn’t win? 
She quietly found out and discovered that some of the members felt bad for her; that if they won, she would have no motorcycle to ride. That was why they didn’t give their awesome ideas. However, when the bike became older, it was then kind of lame if they won the award.
Understanding that, she didn’t promise to give out the fancy awards anymore. This time, she only gave some cute little gifts for good ideas as a gesture of appreciation. Immediately, a flood of emails were sent to her. Here were some brilliant ideas that she received:
- Bring your own reusable shopping bag whenever we go shopping with so that we won’t receive new plastic bags from the store anymore. We even have to hold the shop responsible for keep wastefully and unnecessary give out too many plastics bags for their customers.
 - Always ask the brand of store if they will receive the used plastic bags and used products to recycle or not. If they do not have the clear return-to-recycle policy, we won’t buy their products anymore.
- For the type of waste that can be decomposed on its own, such as organic materials, we will make it compost by adding an enzyme to it. When it turns into fertilizer, we can use that to grow our own clean and organic vegetables. If we cannot consume all that vegetables, give those to our neighbors.
- Keep waste of metal, paper, plastic, glass, etc. and find a recycling center to sell them at a low price of for free.
- Campaign for Congress’s approval for the bill that make manufacturers have to take back the used packaging and products to recycle.
- Create a group that young volunteers could collect many used stuff to fix it and turn it into usable, cheap, and new products such as clothes and furniture. They could sell them for very cheap price to people with low-income, or donate to college students who are in need. 

- There is very little waste that should be burned (better not mention), and we should bring it to the power plants that generate electricity by burning trash.
There were so many brilliant ideas.
After that, the Trash-Lovers Association also proposed another competition to see whose members create the least waste. After three consecutive months of carefully watching the trash can of each member, the jury awarded the first prize to a friend named Great Wisdom because, during that period, he was creating no trash at all.
The head and many members of the association came to give the gifts to Great Wisdom, including a nice flower bouquet, a teddy bear, a box of chocolate with the honoree certificate.
After a small party, everybody left. When they were only a few meters away, they heard some neighbor of Great Wisdom yelling and saying that “... why do you always put your garbage in my trash can…"

43. The Child of Garbage
Luong was picked up from the landfill. However, Luong was not his actual name. The name “Luong” was recommended by an officer who thought that it would be a nicer name compared to Luom, which means “to pick up something,” which is his real name. His parents just named him the way they met and adopted him – picking him up at the garbage dump. His parents worked as scavengers, and they found that he was thrown out when he was born. His parents were not shy about that at all. They kept telling everybody in the neighborhood about that story. Thus, everyone knew about Luom, how he was found, how he was a great kid, and how he deserved to be loved. 
Luong had a sad look on his face because he knew he was once an abandoned child. The desire to find out his biological parents and the hatred towards them are the dilemmas that Luong had for his life. Nevertheless, Luong was a great child, especially to his adopted parents. He was very thoughtful, respectful, and helpful son. All the neighbors always admired his parents for raising and having such a good son. They said that having a adopted child like Luong was even better than the having the biological ones.
Besides that, Luong was the best student of his class, and in his free time, he collected garbage for the school, just like an instinct. His parents were poor but they determined to give him a good education. They had him as an only child, so they loved him with all their hearts. Luong often helped his parents clean, dry, and classify garbage, and the parents would sell it to recycle facility.
After his graduation in the university, Luong wanted to find a better job to support his parents so that they didn’t have to work with trash anymore. However, his school suggested that he should study for a doctorate degree and skip a Master’s because of his excellent results at the university. Luong spent a few days thinking about the offer then asked his parents. His parents didn’t even understand what a doctor was but they encouraged him to go ahead because they were still able to work to support him.
Luong graduated with a dissertation on waste treatment in underdeveloped countries. He got the highest honor (Summa Cum Laude) for his doctorate degree. 
Luong's dissertation was so good and feasible that the State requested the pilot implementation all over the country.
His plan didn’t take away the job of the poor people that usually worked with waste management. If anything, it made them and their job become more valuable in the society. His plan suggested that not everything would turn into waste and garbage. The law required every citizen to reuse almost everything that they bought. Only the certain and limited amount of unusable waste could be sent to the waste treatment facilities to sort and recycle. Those facilities only took and dealt with unusable waste. Thus, it would consume much less resources and energy that ultimately would save a lot of money from the government. 
Luong also conducted a research on an enzyme that could turn organic waste (except plastic waste) into super-clean liquid fertilizer to provide clean vegetable growers.
Under his plan, plastic waste was considered as gold because it could be recycled into many valuable items.
After five years applying his Waste Treatment Project, the national budget and GDP significantly increased. It turned out that garbage and trash were the precious resources if we knew how to use it.
Luong was appointed deputy minister of Environment Ministry. He was the youngest deputy minister of the country. The inauguration day was made by the prime minister, with the attendance of his parents. The parents were so happy and proud. Luong always said that he could made this far only because his parents agreed to sacrifice to work more to support him in studying for the doctorate. 
After shaking hands with all the officials, on the way back to his seat, Luong saw the young man waving at him. Surprisingly, they were as alike as two peas. 
Everyone found that strange thing too.
They both felt the invisible bond between them. Both had tears in their eyes. The young man said:
- Nice to meet you Dr. Luong. My name is Thang. May I ask who are your parents?
Luong pursed his lips and said:
- Sorry, but I’m just a child of garbage.
Luong pushed Thang away and hurried to his parents and took them away, abandoning the party.

44. Naval forces 
- Eagle, the enemy’s UH submarine has entered our waters at a depth of 80m, the grid reference is XYZ, at a speed of 20 knots. Over.
- Z26, let continue to monitor them. Over.
After 30 minutes.
- Eagle, UH came to the grid LMN, at a depth of 30m, at a speed of 10 knots. Over.
- Z26, be ready for action. Over.
- Yes, Eagle. Over.
After 30 minutes.
- Eagle, UH unexpectedly stopped, the grid is CDE, the depth is 20m, perhaps they are about to take action. Please order to attack. Over.
- Ok, Z26. Let attack as planned. Over.
Z26 pushed down the button, and his mini-submarine dived deep underwater, heading to the enemy’s submarine - UH. At about 100m from the sub, Z26 left his boat, carried a torpedo, and swam to the UH.
Unexpectedly, three frogmen came out of the diving chamber with many tools. Z26 quickly hid and moved along with the group of tunas swimming by. The three frogmen swam towards the stern of the ship and fiddled around with the giant propeller.
Z26 was curious about that. He dived deeper and approached them. It turned out that the propeller got stuck in a massive pile of plastic waste. It was locked so hard that the powerful engine of the submarine couldn’t spin it. The captain was afraid that the propeller or the engine would be broken if they kept trying to run the engine. He ordered three robots to untie plastic waste. But because of the severity, it would take more than 60 minutes to finish.
Z26 couldn’t wait long, so he decided to attack. But he didn’t know where he should put the bomb. Z26 suddenly found that he didn’t need a weapon to attack them. The trash was enough to cause the disaster to his enemy. Z26 let go of bomb and it was floating at a depth of 20m. He then drew the knife and quickly swam to the propeller. Those three frogmen didn’t carry underwater weapons, so they were not the opponent of Z26. With a few blows, Z26 could kill all three frogmen. Their blood spread and sharks would smell it and come in five minutes.
Z26 found a lot of plastic waste suspended in this area, so he gathered it to lock the propeller.
When sharks started to arrive at a scene around the submarine, Z26 went inside his mini-sub and went back to his site.
- Eagle, please ordered to torpedo the enemy at the grid of CDE, the UH submarine won’t be able to move in next 30 minutes. The portable torpedo was useless, but the enemy’s submarine was locked successfully. The enemy is drifting underground at a speed of 3 knots. Over.
On the ship, the admiral shook hands with Z26 and awarded him a medal for his successful destruction of the enemy who entered our territorial waters.
Z26 softly said:
- This victory belongs to plastic garbage that floating at a depth of 20m, Sir. The ocean is now filled with plastic garbage.
Admiral frowned:
- So, what if plastic garbage clutches our propellers? Naval courses around the world have never mentioned this.

45. Choose to Stay.
The man and his son came to say farewell to a neighbor.
- Hello, our family are going to the United States tomorrow for settlement. So, today we come to say goodbye to neighbors. We wish you a happy life here. We will miss you dearly! 
The neighbor got surprised. He said:
- Could you two sit down and have a tea with me??
When they sat down, the man asked:
- Which state will you settle in? Why you abandon everything over here to start from the beginning over there?
- We will live in Arizona. I go there because of my children’s future, not for myself. All I want is for my kid to receive an excellent education over there. I will find a job as a sales assistant or a waiter.
- But you are a lecturer at the architectural university over here. What a waste for you to move there and do such jobs…
- Unfortunately, our degrees are not recognized over there, and it’s too late for me to study something new. So I just have to sacrifice my life for my kid’s future.
After a time of keeping silence, the boy cried:
- Today I know the real reason for our settlement. You always tell me that we go there to live near grandparents, and I was so touched by the way you treated your parents. But now I know it is all because of my good education, you choose to abandon your career here and will work as a waiter in the United States. I don’t want to go anymore…

The two men turned to look at the boy in surprise. The boy continued:
- I feel horrible when hearing people despise the education system of Vietnam, so I swear that I will do something to make it prestigious.
